# CATEGORY 2: DEFENSIVE PHILOSOPHIES (3 Trees)

These define HOW a character survives.

---

## TREE 9: ARMORED DEFENSE

**Crux: Armor Training** [Mundane, Passive] (F/C)
Proficiency with armor types. Reduce armor check penalties and speed reduction.

- **Armor Specialization** [Mundane, Passive] (F) — Choose one armor type. Ignore its speed penalty entirely.
- **Armor Mastery** [Mundane, Passive] (F) — Nearly immune to critical hits while wearing chosen armor. Spend Stamina to negate extra crit damage.
- **Stalwart Defense** [Mundane, Active] (F) — When fighting defensively, forgo dodge bonus to gain equal Soak (DR). *(Note: distinct from Fort/Will Evasion talent, see Durability tree)*

---

## TREE 10: AGILE DEFENSE

**Crux: Uncanny Dodge** [Mundane, Passive] (F/C)
Cannot be caught flat-footed. React to danger before senses would normally allow.

- **Evasion** [Mundane, Passive] (F/C) — On a successful Reflex save for half damage, take no damage instead. *(Requires light or no armor)*
- **Improved Uncanny Dodge** [Mundane, Passive] (F/C) — Cannot be flanked.
- **Improved Evasion** [Mundane, Passive] (F) — On a failed Reflex save for half damage, take half damage. On success, take none.

---

## TREE 11: DURABILITY

**Crux: Toughness** [Mundane, Passive] (F)
Bonus hit points per level.

### Unstoppable Sub-Tree:
- **Diehard** [Mundane, Passive] (F) — Automatically stabilize when reduced to negative HP. Continue acting while disabled.
- **Deathless Master** [Mundane, Passive] (F) — Continue fighting below 0 HP without penalty until truly dead.

### Endurance Sub-Tree:
- **Endurance** [Mundane, Passive] (F) — Bonus to saves against exhaustion, fatigue, environmental conditions.
- **Heroic Recovery** [Mundane, Active] (F) — Spend Stamina to immediately end one condition (fatigued, shaken, sickened).
- **Iron Resolve** [Mundane, Reactive] (C: Samurai) — As an immediate action, spend Stamina to negate one condition for 1 round or remove one minor condition entirely. ✅ PLACED

### Fortified Resolve (Fort/Will Evasion):
- **Fortified Resolve** [Mundane, Passive] (C: Inquisitor) — When you succeed on a Fortitude or Will save against an effect with reduced effect on success, you instead suffer no effect. *(Parallel to Evasion in Agile Defense, keyed to different saves. Distinct from Stalwart Defense in Armored tree.)* ✅ PLACED

### Rage Stance (Sub-Tree within Durability):
- **Primal Rage** [Mundane, Stance] (F/C) — Enter a rage state gaining bonuses to Strength-based attacks, damage, and Will saves, at the cost of defense penalties. Cannot use skills requiring concentration. Ends when combat ends or voluntarily ended as free action.
  - *Rage-Strain Interaction Rule:* While in Rage stance, effective Strain threshold for all isotopes is reduced by [Rage tier × modifier]. This makes the character more permeable to planar expression during rage. (C: Bloodrager)
- **Terrifying Rage** [Mundane, Passive] (F) — While Primal Rage is active, bonus to Intimidate checks.
- **Fearsome Howl** [Mundane, Active] (F) — While raging, spend Stamina to force all enemies in 30ft to save vs fear.
- **Tireless Rage** [Mundane, Passive] (F) — No fatigue when rage ends.
- **Superstitious** [Mundane, Passive] (F) — While raging, bonus to saves against spells and supernatural abilities.
- **Come and Get Me** [Mundane, Stance] (F) — While raging, enemies gain bonus to attack you, but each hit provokes a free counterattack from you.

### Totemic Path (Sub-Tree, Rage-dependent):
- **Beast Totem (Lesser)** [Supernatural, Passive] (F/C) — While raging, gain natural claw attacks. *(Verdant Font Fauna isotope expression activated by Rage Strain threshold reduction)*
- **Beast Totem** [Supernatural, Passive] (F/C) — While raging, gain natural armor bonus. *(Fauna isotope defensive expression)*
- **Beast Totem (Greater)** [Supernatural, Active] (F/C) — While raging, gain pounce (full attack on charge). *(Fauna isotope movement expression)*

⚠️ **FLAG: Totemic Path talents are tagged [Supernatural] — they are mutation candidates for the later separation pass. They remain here as scaffolding.**

---

# CATEGORY 3: TACTICAL / GENERAL (7+ Trees)

These define WHAT you do with your combat actions beyond basic attacking and defending.

---

## TREE 12: COMBAT MANEUVERS (Meta-Tree with 10+ Sub-Trees)

Each combat maneuver has its own Improved → Greater chain. These are listed by maneuver type.

### Trip:
- **Improved Trip** [Mundane, Active] (F) — No AoO provoked. Bonus to Trip checks.
- **Greater Trip** [Mundane, Active] (F) — Target provokes AoO from allies when tripped.

### Grapple:
- **Improved Grapple** [Mundane, Active] (F) — No AoO. Bonus to Grapple checks.
- **Greater Grapple** [Mundane, Active] (F) — Maintain grapple as move action.
- **Chokehold** [Mundane, Active] (F) — While grappling, can cut off air supply.
- **Body Shield** [Mundane, Active] (F) — Use grappled opponent as cover.

### Disarm:
- **Improved Disarm** [Mundane, Active] (F) — No AoO. Bonus to Disarm checks.
- **Greater Disarm** [Mundane, Active] (F) — Disarmed weapon lands 15 feet away.
- **Disarming Strike** [Mundane, Active] (F) — Free Disarm on a critical hit.

### Sunder:
- **Improved Sunder** [Mundane, Active] (F) — No AoO. Bonus to Sunder checks.
- **Greater Sunder** [Mundane, Active] (F) — Excess damage carries through to wielder.
- **Shattering Strike** [Mundane, Active] (F) — Bonus to Sunder against specific material types.

### Shove (Bull Rush):
- **Improved Shove** [Mundane, Active] (F) — No AoO. Bonus to Shove checks.
- **Greater Shove** [Mundane, Active] (F) — Target provokes AoO from allies when shoved.
- **Hurricane Punch** [Mundane, Active] (F) — Free Shove on successful unarmed attack.

### Feint:
- **Improved Feint** [Mundane, Active] (F) — Feint as a move action instead of standard.
- **Greater Feint** [Mundane, Active] (F) — Feinted target is flat-footed to all attackers, not just you.

### Dirty Trick:
- **Improved Dirty Trick** [Mundane, Active] (F) — No AoO. Bonus to Dirty Trick checks.
- **Greater Dirty Trick** [Mundane, Active] (F) — Condition from Dirty Trick lasts additional rounds.

### Drag:
- **Improved Drag** [Mundane, Active] (F) — No AoO. Bonus to Drag checks.

### Reposition:
- **Improved Reposition** [Mundane, Active] (F) — No AoO. Bonus to Reposition checks.

### Steal:
- **Improved Steal** [Mundane, Active] (F) — No AoO. Bonus to Steal checks.

*Note: All Combat Maneuver talents scale with item tier (weapon properties that grant maneuver bonuses scale at Novice/Journeyman/Master from the SRD v2 weapon property system).*

---

## TREE 13: CRITICAL

Two branches: Precision (surgical) and Brutal (overwhelming).

### Precision Branch:
- **Critical Focus** [Mundane, Passive] (F) — Bonus to critical confirmation rolls.
- **Bleeding Critical** [Mundane, Passive] (F) — Confirmed crits cause bleed damage.
- **Staggering Critical** [Mundane, Passive] (F) — Confirmed crits stagger the target.
- **Disarming Strike** [Mundane, Active] (F) — Free Disarm on critical. *(Cross-tags: [Combat Maneuver (Disarm)])*

### Brutal Branch:
- **Impact Critical** [Mundane, Passive] (F) — Crits with bludgeoning weapons push target back.
- **Impaling Critical** [Mundane, Passive] (F) — Crits with piercing weapons impale, dealing ongoing damage.
- **Exploit Weakness** [Mundane, Passive] (C: Samurai/high-tier) — Critical hits ignore Soak (DR).

---

## TREE 14: COMMANDER / AURA

Three branches: Vow of Enmity, Battlefield Tactician, Aura of Command.

### Vow of Enmity Branch (5 entry points, shared progression):

**Entry Points (choose one — determines source and keying attribute):**
- **Smite (Divine Mark)** [Infused, Swift] (C: Paladin) — Mark fueled by divine isotope resonance. Key: Schema divine score 60+.
- **Quarry (Instinctual Mark)** [Mundane, Swift] (C: Ranger) — Mark from creature/terrain knowledge. Key: Wits/Intellect + Knowledge ranks.
- **Challenge (Emotional Mark)** [Mundane, Swift] (C: Cavalier) — Mark from personal honor commitment. Key: Presence/Composure.
- **Studied Combat (Analytical Mark)** [Mundane, Move→Swift] (C: Investigator) — Mark from real-time tactical analysis. Key: Intellect + Knowledge check. *(Quick Study droplet reduces action to Swift.)*
- **Bane (Categorical Mark)** [Infused, Swift] (C: Inquisitor) — Mark applied to entire creature type via Knowledge check. Breadth trades for reduced per-target intensity.

**Shared Progression (all entry points feed into):**
- **Tier 2 — Commander's Judgment** [Mundane/Infused, Passive] (F/C) — Vow also analyzes target's defenses. Ignore a portion of target's Soak.
- **Tier 3 — Overwhelming Assault** [Mundane/Infused, Active] (F/C) — Allies also gain a portion of your attack bonus against the Vow target.
- **Capstone — Unbreakable Vow** [Mundane/Infused, Passive] (F/C) — Maintain two Vows simultaneously. Regain Stamina on defeating a Vow target.

### Battlefield Tactician Branch:
- **Coordinated Action (Tier 1)** [Mundane, Passive] (F/C) — Master one Teamwork talent. Always considered to have an ally with that talent for activation purposes.
- **Tactician (Tier 2)** [Mundane, Active] (F/C) — Spend Stamina to grant one Teamwork talent to allies within 30ft for several rounds.
- **Solo Tactics / Tactical Intuition (Tier 3)** [Mundane, Passive] (F/C: Inquisitor/Cavalier) — BIDIRECTIONAL: Your Teamwork talents function as if adjacent allies possess them, AND you possess any Teamwork talent an adjacent ally has. ✅ MERGED
- **Master Tactician (Capstone)** [Mundane, Swift] (F/C) — Use Tactician as a swift action. Share two Teamwork talents simultaneously.

### Aura of Command Branch:
- **Aura of Courage (Tier 1)** [Mundane/Infused, Passive] (F/C) — 10ft aura. Allies gain bonus on saves vs fear.
- **Banner (Tier 2)** [Mundane, Active] (F/C) — Plant a banner creating a larger aura with attack or save bonuses.
- **Inspiring Presence (Tier 3)** [Infused, Active] (F/C) — Spend Stamina per round. Allies gain scaling bonus to attack, damage, or skill checks.
- **Stance Projection (Capstone)** [Infused, Active] (C: Skald) — Project your current active Stance to willing allies within aura radius. Allies gain the stance's benefits and penalties. Can accept or decline each round. ✅ MERGED (replaces specific "Raging Song" capstone with generalized version)
- **Contingent Expression** [Magical, Active — Prep Phase] (C: Mesmerist) — During rest, spend ÆP to implant one triggered expression in a willing ally. Fires automatically when conditions are met. Uses YOUR stats. Maintain up to Dominion modifier simultaneously. ✅ PLACED

---

## TREE 15: INTIMIDATION & FEAR

- **Dazzling Display (Crux)** [Mundane, Active] (F) — Full action. Intimidate all enemies within 30ft using a weapon display.
- **Cornugon Smash** [Mundane, Active] (F) — Free Intimidate on a Power Attack hit. *(Cross-tags: [Two-Handed Style])*
- **Shatter Defenses** [Mundane, Passive] (F) — Frightened or shaken targets are flat-footed to your attacks.
- **Gory Finish** [Mundane, Active] (F) — When you drop a foe, free Intimidate vs all enemies who witnessed it.
- **Fearsome Might** [Mundane, Passive] (F) — Use Strength instead of Charisma for Intimidate checks.
- **Dreadful Carnage** [Mundane, Active] (F) — When you reduce a foe to 0 HP, free Dazzling Display.
- **Intimidating Prowess** [Mundane, Passive] (F) — Add Strength modifier to Intimidate checks.

---

## TREE 16: PRECISION DAMAGE

**Crux: Sneak Attack / Precision Damage** [Mundane, Active] (F/C)
Deal extra precision damage dice when target is flanked, flat-footed, or otherwise denied defenses.

- **Analytical Approach** [Mundane, Move→Swift] (C: Investigator) — Alternative activation: study a target (Knowledge check + move action) to apply Precision Damage without requiring flanking or surprise. Quick Study droplet reduces to swift. ✅ PLACED
- **Studied Strike** [Mundane, Active] (C: Investigator) — Cash out Analytical Approach for burst precision damage, ending the study. *(Re-studying costs Inspiration/FP.)*
- **Debilitating Strike** [Mundane, Active] (F/C) — When you deal Precision Damage, spend Stamina to apply a debilitating penalty (speed, attacks, or defense).
- **Bleeding Attack** [Mundane, Passive] (F/C) — Precision Damage causes bleed.
- **Crippling Strike** [Mundane, Active] (F) — Precision Damage also applies Strength damage.
- **Master Strike** [Mundane, Active] (F/C) — Capstone. Once per day, Precision Damage forces save vs paralysis or death.

---

## TREE 17: TEAMWORK

Talents that require coordination. Solo Tactics / Tactical Intuition (Commander tree) allows these to function without trained allies.

- **Paired Opportunists** [Mundane, Passive] (F) — When ally provokes AoO from a flanked enemy, you also get an AoO.
- **Precise Strike (Teamwork)** [Mundane, Passive] (F) — When flanking, deal extra precision damage. *(Distinct from Daredevil's Precise Strike.)*
- **Outflank** [Mundane, Passive] (F) — Increased flanking bonus. On a crit, flanking partner gets an AoO.
- **Broken Wing Gambit** [Mundane, Reactive] (F) — Take a penalty to defense. When enemy hits you, all allies with this talent get an AoO.
- **Coordinated Charge** [Mundane, Reactive] (F) — When an ally charges, you can immediately charge the same target.
- **Swap Places** [Mundane, Active] (F) — Switch positions with adjacent ally.
- **Escape Route** [Mundane, Passive] (F) — No AoO for movement when adjacent to ally with this talent.

## TREE 18: AREA ATTACK

*(Note: Also functions as a sub-tree off Power Attack in Two-Handed Style. Cross-referenced here for characters who access it through other paths.)*

# CATEGORY 4: ESOTERIC / RESOURCE (Revised)

These trees bridge martial and magical investment, govern resource models, and handle non-standard combat systems.

---

## TREE 19: CHANNELING DISCIPLINE (NEW — from class extractions)

Talents governing HOW magical expressions are delivered, recovered, and amplified. These are learned techniques, not innate abilities. Feed Tₘ.

### Conduit Channeling Path (C: Kineticist):
- **Conduit Channeling (Crux)** [Magical, Active] (N) — Accept voluntary Strain on a known isotope to reduce ÆP/Stamina cost of an expression using that isotope. Trade long-term mutation risk for short-term power.
- **Planar Reserve** [Magical, Passive] (N) — During rest, pre-process and store Strain points equal to (Integrity modifier). Stored points can be spent in place of accepting live Strain.
- **Planar Attunement** [Magical, Passive] (N) — While your Strain counter on any isotope is above 0, gain scaling bonus to expressions using that isotope. Higher Strain = bigger bonus = closer to mutation threshold.
- **Focused Channeling** [Magical, Active] (N) — Spend a move/standard action to reduce ÆP or Stamina cost of your next expression by a scaling amount. If you take damage while gathering, concentration check or forced Strain.
- **Ambient Expression** [Magical, Passive] (N) — When you have Strain on an isotope AND that isotope is at rank 7+, passively emanate the isotope's interaction profile in a small radius. Allies and enemies affected. High-tier talent.
- **Overexpression** [Magical, Active] (N) — Exceed the normal ÆP/Strain limits of a single expression for dramatically increased effect. Temporarily severs connection to that isotope until re-established (1 action). Capstone.

### Channeled Strike Path (C: Magus):
- **Channeled Strike (Crux)** [Magical, Active] (N) — Deliver one expression through a melee weapon attack using the weapon's attack roll. Expression must use an isotope at rank 3+. Cost: expression's ÆP + Stamina surcharge by ESL. Requires reset action before reuse. On crit, expression also crits.
- **Resonant Stance** [Magical, Stance] (N) — After Channeled Strike or casting, enter stance. Weapon strikes deal bonus damage equal to (isotope rank / 2) of the last channeled isotope's damage type.
- **Improvised Infusion** [Magical, Swift] (N) — Spend ÆP to temporarily apply one expression effect to weapon. Duration: minutes = isotope rank. No slot occupied. *(Subsumes Arcane Pool weapon enhancement.)*
- **Flowing Channel** [Magical, Passive] (N) — When you cast an expression with ESL 2 or lower through normal casting, Channeled Strike recharges immediately without reset action.
- **Reactive Channel** [Magical, Reactive] (N) — When a creature you threaten begins an expression, spend reaction + Stamina for Channeled Strike against them. On hit + damage, target must concentrate or lose expression. High-tier, requires Combat Reflexes.
- **Ætheric Charge** [Magical, Active] (N) — Channeled Strike variant during Charge action. Bonus damage from momentum. *(Subsumed from standalone feat-derived talent.)*

### General Channeling Talents:
- **Disciplined Focus** [Magical, Active] (C: Psionics) — Spend full-round action to achieve focused state. While focused: passive bonus to one defense pillar = Integrity modifier. Expend focus (free action) for one of: add Integrity to a d20 roll, negate a condition for 1 round, or reduce next expression's ÆP cost by Integrity. Multiple talents can read the focused/unfocused state.
- **Reckless Channeling** [Magical, Active] (C: Psionics/Wilder) — Increase effective ESL cap by 1-3 beyond Tri-Tier limit. Per point of increase: cumulative 15% chance of Channeling Backlash (dazed 1 round, lose ÆP, expend Focus).
- **Swift Self-Channel** [Magical, Swift] (C: Warpriest) — Cast a self-targeted expression as a swift action by spending additional ÆP equal to the expression's ESL.
- **Reactive Modification** [Magical, Active — at moment of casting] (C: Psychic) — Maintain a secondary pool (Integrity mod + relevant attribute mod) spent ONLY at moment of expression release. Each point applies one minor modification from a menu determined by isotope profile (maximize one die, self-heal, persist effect, extend targeting, add secondary damage type).
- **Practiced Rote Mastery** [Magical, Passive] (C: Witch) — Signature Rotes with ESL 2 or below: ÆP cost drops to 0. Each Mastered Rote can only affect a specific target once per day.
- **Rote Compression** [Magical, Passive] (C: Psychic) — Voluntarily reduce a Signature Rote's ESL by 1-3 steps, proportionally reducing ÆP cost and effect power. Deploy high-investment rotes flexibly across the power spectrum.
- **Wandering Attunement** [Magical, Active — Prep Phase] (C: Shaman) — During rest, temporarily attune to one isotope you DON'T have ranked, treating it as rank 1 for the day. Gain access to basic expressions and one Mastered Rote from its natural effect list. No Strain, no mutation. Prerequisite: any two isotopes at rank 5+.

---

## TREE 20: METAMAGIC (from Book of Shadows)

Each is a learned technique that modifies expression construction. All cost additional ÆP. All feed Tₘ.

- **Empower Spell** [Magical, Active] (B) — +2 ÆP. Increase all variable numeric effects by 50%.
- **Maximize Spell** [Magical, Active] (B) — +4 ÆP. All variable numeric effects at maximum value.
- **Quicken Spell** [Magical, Active] (B) — +(ESL × 2) ÆP. Cast standard-action expression as swift.
- **Enlarge Spell** [Magical, Active] (B) — +1 ÆP. Double range.
- **Widen Spell** [Magical, Active] (B) — +2 ÆP. Double area of effect.
- **Extend Spell** [Magical, Active] (B) — +1 ÆP. Double duration.
- **Unbalanced Magic** [Magical, Active] (B) — Two maneuvers: Overwhelm (penalty to attack, equal bonus to DC/damage) or Pinpoint (penalty to damage/DC, equal bonus to attack).

*Architect Efficiency:* Metamagic surcharges reduced by ⌊Intellect ÷ 2⌋ each, minimum +1 per metamagic. (B)
*Improviser Affinity:* Base ÆP per spell reduced by (Anima - 3), minimum 0. Metamagic surcharges paid at full rate. (B)

---

## TREE 21: FOCUS TALENTS (from Book of Shadows)

Passive specialization bonuses. Feed Tₘ.

- **Focus (Effect)** [Magical, Passive] (B) — Choose one Effect (e.g., Affliction). All expressions using this Effect have +1 save DC. Can be taken multiple times.
- **Focus (Subtype/Isotope)** [Magical, Passive] (B) — Choose one isotope. All expressions using this isotope have +1 save DC. Can be taken multiple times.
- **Spell Penetration** [Magical, Passive] (B) — Bonus on checks to overcome magical resistance or Nullify effects.
- **Warden Breaker** [Magical, Passive] (B) — Significant bonus to overcome static magical defenses (Nullify effects, Deepguard wards).
- **Immunity Breach** [Magical, Active] (B) — Prerequisite: high Overall Mastery. Spend significant additional ÆP to temporarily suppress a creature's Immunity to a specific isotope for one round.
- **Expanded Consciousness** [Magical, Passive] (B) — Adds (OMT × 3) to Mastery Capacity Pool. Can be purchased up to OMT times. *(Governs companion capacity, sustained expression capacity, Identity Manifestation capacity.)*

---

## TREE 22: MUTATION (Scaffolding — contents are future mutation catalogue candidates)

### Primal Aspect (C: Hunter):
- **Primal Aspect** [Supernatural, Swift] (N) — Verdant Font Fauna isotope at rank 3+. Manifest minor physical adaptations as a swift action. Choose from menu determined by Fauna isotope rank. Switch as swift action. At rank 7+, manifest two simultaneously. Entry point for voluntary temporary micro-mutation. ⚠️ [Supernatural] tag — mutation catalogue candidate.

### Innate Expression (C: Oracle — Character Creation, not talent tree):
- **Innate Expression** [Supernatural, Passive] (N) — Character creation option. Permanent, irremovable paired detriment/benefit. Detriment constant; benefit scales with investment thresholds. Generates 1 FP per session as pattern break. Produces a mandatory behavioral signature (leitmotif). ⚠️ [Supernatural] tag — mutation catalogue candidate.

### Identity Manifestation (C: Psionics):
- **Identity Manifestation** [Supernatural, Swift] (N) — Designate one known expression (typically Summon weapon/armor) as an Identity Manifestation. ÆP cost permanently 0. Call/dismiss as swift action. Scales with isotope rank + effect tier. No material substrate (no slots, no routing). Can only have ONE. Prerequisite: Summon effect Tier 3+, relevant isotope rank 5+. ⚠️ [Supernatural] tag — mutation catalogue candidate.

*Note: All Totemic Path talents (Durability tree) are also [Supernatural] tagged and will transfer to the mutation catalogue in the separation pass.*

---

## TREE 23: SŌMATURGY (Partially deferred)

Contains the self-transformation and external construct systems. Identified Crux paths:

- **Forge Construct (Crux)** [Magical/Supernatural] (F/C) — Create an external construct companion via Summon effect. Evolution Pool for customization. *(Overlaps with Companion system, Summons acquisition path.)*
- **Shape Soulmeld (Crux)** [Magical/Supernatural] (F/C) — Invest Stamina into semi-permanent self-enhancements (Soulmelds) that occupy body slots. From Akashic Mysteries source.
- **Spell Combat integration** — 🔧 How does casting + fighting in the same turn work at the system level? Channeled Strike (Magus extraction) provides the weapon-delivery path. But what about casting a non-touch expression + making a weapon attack in the same round? This is the unresolved action economy question.

## TREE 24: COMPANION SYSTEM (Two-Axis)

### Axis 1 — Acquisition Path (choose one):

**Contracts (Binding):**
- **Magical Contract (Crux)** [Magical, Active — Ritual] (Companion Doc) — Create magically binding contracts. Cost: Stamina + ÆP = target EML. Counts against Mastery Capacity Pool.
- Ironclad Oath, Binding Mark, Contractual Sense (Novice) (Companion Doc)
- Strengthened Chains, Binding Circle (Adept) (Companion Doc)
- Unbreakable Vow (Master) (Companion Doc)
- Contractual Enhancement tree (Companion Doc)
- Contract Versatility tree (Companion Doc)

**Empathy (Bonding):**
- **Empathetic Bond (Crux)** [Mundane→Magical, Active — Ritual] (Companion Doc) — Form permanent empathetic bond with willing creature. Silent communication of emotions and simple concepts.
- Bond Enhancement tree (Companion Doc)
- Shared Abilities tree (Companion Doc)

**Psychic Contagion (needs rename):**
- **Mental Domination (Crux)** [Magical, Active] (Companion Doc) — Compel creature through psychic force. ⚠️ Ethically restricted under guild law for sapient creatures.

**Summons:**
- **Conjured Companion (Crux)** [Magical, Active] (Companion Doc) — Magically create or call a companion. Includes Construct Customization (C: Summoner) — apply mutations from menu determined by isotope investment, reconfigurable on resummon.

### Axis 2 — Creature Type Specialization (choose one+):
Beast (Wild), Arcaniform (Arcanist), Anomalous (Harbinger), Floraform (Verdant), Forged (Artificer), Humanoid (Command), Interstitial (Binder), Returned (Gravemaster)

*(Each has Novice/Adept/Master talent tiers per the Companion Archetype document.)*

### Cross-Path Talents (available to any acquisition path):
- **Proxy Channeling** [Magical, Active] (C: Wizard/Witch) — Deliver touch-range expressions through companion. Uses YOUR stats, companion's position.
- **Sense Sharing** [Magical, Active] (C: Wizard/Witch/Psionics) — Perceive through companion's senses.
- **Synthesis** [Supernatural, Active] (C: Summoner/Spiritualist) — Merge companion into self. Gain subset of companion's Feature Point investments as personal bonuses. ⚠️ [Supernatural] — mutation catalogue candidate.
- **Full Synthesis** [Supernatural, Active] (C: Summoner) — Capstone. Transform into hybrid of self and companion. ⚠️ [Supernatural] — mutation catalogue candidate.
- **Handler's Shield** [Mundane, Reactive] — Take damage that would hit your companion instead.
- **Living Archive** [Magical, Passive] (C: Witch/Shaman) — Companion stores rotes/reagents/isotope expressions. Access through bond. Lose access if companion lost.
- **Companion Resonance** [Magical/Mundane, Passive] — Passive skill/save bonus while companion is within 30ft. Bonus determined by creature type.
- **Focus Sharing** [Magical/Mundane, Active] (C: Hunter) — Apply your active Primal Aspect or self-buff to companion.
- **Teamwork Sharing** [Mundane, Passive] (C: Hunter) — Companion benefits from your Teamwork talents as if it possessed them.
- **Raise Companion** [Magical, Active — Ritual] (C: Hunter) — Spend significant resources to revive dead bonded companion through the bond.
- **Dual-State Companion** [Supernatural, Swift] (C: Spiritualist) — Toggle companion between incorporeal and physical states. ⚠️ [Supernatural] — mutation catalogue candidate.

# CATEGORY 5: GENERAL POOL

Talents that don't belong to a specific tree. Available to any character meeting prerequisites.

---

## Daredevil Engine (Grit & Panache):
- **Grit & Panache (Crux)** [Mundane, Passive] (F/C) — Gain a Stamina Pool. Core methods of regaining Stamina: critical hits and defeating foes. Entry point for the Deed system.
- **Charmed Life** [Mundane, Reactive] (F/C) — Spend Stamina as reaction to add bonus to any saving throw.
- **Cheat Death** [Mundane, Reactive] (F/C) — When reduced to 0 HP, spend significant Stamina to be reduced to 1 HP instead.
- **Deeds (à la carte):** Each is a separate talent purchased individually.
  - *Offensive:* Deadeye (ranged touch), Precise Strike (finesse damage), Targeting Shot (debuff specific body part)
  - *Defensive:* Opportune Parry & Riposte (see Finesse tree), Gunslinger's Dodge
  - *Mobility:* Kip-Up (stand from prone as swift)

⚠️ **FLAG: The Daredevil engine has been wanting its own tree since the April 10 session. It's currently in General Pool but may deserve promotion to a 9th Foundational Style or a 4th Defensive Philosophy. The Stamina recovery-on-performance mechanic is unique enough to warrant independent tree status.**

## Invoked Aspect (C: Inquisitor):
- **Invoked Aspect** [Magical/Infused, Swift] (N) — Invoke one aspect of a god whose Schema dimension you score 60+ on. Scaling combat modifier appropriate to that god's portfolio. Switch as swift action. Menu derived from YOUR Schema scores. At higher tiers: invoke two, then three aspects simultaneously. ✅ INDEPENDENT — no overlap with existing feat-derived talents.

## Wielder Attunement (C: Occultist):
- **Wielder Attunement** [Magical, Active — Prep Phase] (N) — During rest, invest ÆP from personal pool into items you possess. Invested ÆP produces resonant bonus determined by item's dominant isotope profile. Supplements item's drip baseline.
- **Field Reallocation** [Magical, Active] (N) — Transfer invested ÆP between items. 10 minutes, 1-point loss per transfer.

## Legacy Channeling (C: Medium):
- **Legacy Channeling (Crux)** [Magical, Active — Ritual] (N) — 1-hour ritual. Attune to a soul stone containing another entity's experiential record. Gain limited access to one category of the channeled soul's talent investment (at reduced effectiveness) for the day. Prerequisite: Palimpsest Echo isotope rank 5+, possession of viable soul stone.
- **Legacy Accord** [Magical, Passive] (N) — When channeling, adopt one of the channeled soul's Instincts. While honored: gain (Integrity mod) free Legacy Surges without contamination. Violation: lose all remaining free surges + immediate contamination.
- *Schema Contamination* — inherent risk mechanic, not a talent. Each heavy draw advances contamination. Thresholds: behavioral bleed → temporary possession.
- *Legacy Surge* — inherent to Legacy Channeling. Add channeled soul's relevant Schema die to a d20 check. Each use advances contamination by 1.

## Analytical Focus / FP Distance 0 (C: Investigator):
- **Professional Expertise** [Mundane, Passive] (N) — FP cost for Distance 0 operations in trained skill domains drops to 0. Add relevant Schema dimension die to personal d20 checks in areas of professional competence at no FP cost. Prerequisite: 5+ ranks in any skill. ✅ PLACED — clarifies the FP Distance 0 mechanic.

## Constructed Identity (C: Vigilante):
- **Constructed Identity** [Mundane, Active] (N) — Maintain a secondary social profile (Poise, Clout, reputation) mechanically separate from primary. Social consequences accumulate independently. Switching: 10 minutes + disguise prep. Investigation TN based on Skullduggery skill. Prerequisite: Skullduggery 5+, Deception 5+. ✅ PLACED — social pillar talent.

## Exploit Weakness / Kirin Strike:
- **Exploit Weakness** [Mundane, Swift] (F) — Make a Knowledge check against a visible creature. On success: add Intellect modifier to damage against that creature for 1 round.

## Weapon Training:
- **Weapon Training** [Mundane, Passive] (F/C) — Choose a weapon group. Scaling Competence bonus to attack and damage. Tiers: Focus → Specialization → Greater → Mastery.

## Martial Flexibility (C: Brawler):
- **Barroom Brawler (Tier 1)** [Mundane, Active] (F/C) — Once per day, spend Stamina to temporarily gain one martial talent you don't possess but meet prerequisites for. Short duration.
- **Martial Flexibility (Tier 2)** [Mundane, Active] (F/C) — More frequent use, longer duration.
- **Effortless Flexibility (Capstone)** [Mundane, Swift] (F/C) — Use as swift action. Adapt fighting style mid-combat.

## Casting Methodology (System Rule, not a talent):
Four channeling methods with different detection and vulnerability profiles:
- **External** — Visible gestures, audible incantations. Blocked by restraint/silence.
- **Internal** — No external tells. Blocked by emotional suppression/mental disruption.
- **Somatic** — Physical motion integrated into combat. Blocked by immobilization.
- **Substrate** — Routed through weapon/implement. Blocked by disarming/sundering.

---

# CATEGORY 6: ARTIFICE PATHS (from SRD v2)

Five crafting talent paths, each with Novice/Journeyman/Master tiers. Already defined in SRD v2 Prima Materia.

- **Path 1: Mundane Artisan** (S) — Physical operations. Masterwork → Efficient Production → Signature Creation.
- **Path 2: Appraiser** (S) — Analysis and valuation. Material Reading → Component Analysis → Full Spectrum Analysis.
- **Path 3: Ætheric Scrivener** (S) — Documentation and inscription. Scribe's Hand → Runic Inscription → Schematics Master.
- **Path 4: Alchemist** (S) — Alchemical operations. Reagent Brewer → Process Alchemist → Essence Alchemist.
- **Path 5: Enchanter** (S) — Component installation and routing. Artificer's Eye → Architecture Design → Forge Architect.

*(Full mechanical details in SRD v2 Prima Materia. Not duplicated here.)*

# THE ÆTHERIUM CODEX — PRODUCTION FRAMEWORK
## Scope-Driven Content Architecture and Market Strategy
### Working Document — April 17, 2026

---

## PURPOSE

This document applies the Ætherium Codex's own Scope system to its production timeline, content architecture, and market deployment strategy. The system describes itself — the tools used to build Tessera's fictional world are the same tools used to plan, produce, and release the real-world product line.

---

# PART I: THE SELF-DESCRIBING PRODUCTION MODEL

## The Production Timeline IS a Scope Stack

The production of the Ætherium Codex is itself an S8 clock. Each product released is a tick on a lower Scope clock within it. The actual play session is an S0 tick. The adventure module is an S2-S3 tick. The faction book is an S5-S7 tick. They all advance the same S8 clock — Tessera's living timeline — from different Scope levels simultaneously.

Production is planned the way a GM plans a campaign. The S8 "story" of Tessera is the business plan. The S5-S7 faction books are quarterly milestones. The S0-S3 actual play sessions are weekly deliverables. Each fills clocks at its own Scope while contributing to higher-level clocks above it.

## The Production Clock

| Scope | Production Element | Tempo | Output |
|---|---|---|---|
| **S0** | Individual Twitch session | 2-4 hours | One live play episode. S0-S3 engagement visible to audience. |
| **S1** | Weekly content cycle | 1 week | Edited episode + social media + community engagement. |
| **S2** | Monthly content batch | 1 month | Adventure arc completion. Podcast/audio drama segment. |
| **S3** | Quarterly milestone | 3 months | Module release, faction document, Patreon exclusive lore card set (30-day exclusivity window). |
| **S4** | Semi-annual product | 6 months | Major supplement. Setting guide. Companion volume. |
| **S5** | Annual anchor | 1 year | Core rulebook edition. Major campaign setting release. |
| **S6** | Multi-year arc | 2-3 years | Complete campaign arc. Setting timeline advancement. |
| **S7** | Product line lifecycle | 5-10 years | Full Tessera product ecosystem. Multiple campaigns, multiple media formats, established audience. |
| **S8** | The project itself | Lifetime | The Ætherium Codex as a body of work. |

Each lower Scope fills clocks that advance the higher Scopes. An S0 Twitch session produces content that feeds the S1 weekly cycle. Monthly batches produce quarterly milestones. Quarterly milestones accumulate toward annual anchors.

---

# PART II: THE CHEKHOV'S GUN ARCHITECTURE

## Building Outside-In, Deploying Bottom-Up

By building the setting and narrative from S8 downward (outside-in), every piece of S0-S3 content can contain structural connections to the larger story. These connections are planted deliberately, not improvised.

The audience discovers them bottom-up. They watch an S0 Twitch session and encounter a throwaway NPC name. Six months later, that name appears on a faction document in a Patreon lore card. A year later, it's a major character in a campaign supplement. The connection was always there. The audience discovers it on their own timeline.

**The Blazing Saddles Principle:** Bury the meaningful detail in plain sight. If you don't know what you're looking at, the rest of the content is still entertaining. If you DO know, the discovery is electrifying. This turns passive consumers into active investigators — the Star Wars frame-by-frame analysis community, but for a TTRPG setting.

**The structural advantage:** Because the S8 story is designed first, every S0 detail CAN connect to it. Not every detail MUST — some things are just fun encounters. But the capacity for hidden depth is built into the architecture rather than retrofitted. Marvel didn't plan the first Infinity Stone appearance. You can.

---

# PART III: MULTI-MEDIA SCOPE DISTRIBUTION

## Different Media Carry Different Scope Levels

The Ætherium Codex is not a single product — it is a content ecosystem where different media formats carry different portions of the Scope stack to different audiences at different temporal resolutions.

| Medium | Scope Range | Audience Engagement | Temporal Resolution |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Twitch / Live Actual Play** | S0-S3 | Live, interactive, campfire | Real-time. The audience watches decisions happen at S0 tempo. The "new campfire" where people engage AS content is produced. |
| **Podcast / Audio Drama** | S0-S4 | Edited, narrative, commute-length | Compressed. Hours of play edited to 30-60 minute episodes. Internal pacing controlled by production. |
| **Video / Edited Content** | S0-S5 | Visual, produced, shareable | Curated. Highlight reels, lore explainers, setting tours. Each piece is a crafted window into a specific Scope level. |
| **Text Adventure / Interactive Fiction** | S0-S4 | Solo, exploratory, personal | Player-paced. The reader controls temporal resolution. Branching narratives that explore the Scope stack through personal choice. |
| **Journal Entries / In-Character Documents** | S0-S6 | Immersive, collectible, lore-dense | Asynchronous. Faction reports, personal letters, institutional records. The Patreon exclusive content tier. |
| **Campaign Modules / Adventure Paths** | S2-S5 | GM-facing, playable, structured | Session-paced. The GM deploys content over weeks to months of play. |
| **Faction Books / Setting Supplements** | S5-S7 | Reference, worldbuilding, inspirational | Slow. Read and absorbed over weeks. Provides the institutional context that makes campaign modules meaningful. |
| **Core Rulebook** | All Scopes | Comprehensive, foundational | The anchor. Contains the full framework at every Scope level but entered from the player's S0 experience. |
| **Kids' Game** | S0 | Accessible, simple, introductory | Immediate. The simplest possible entry point. Learn the setting through play without the full mechanical depth. |

**The critical insight:** All of these media are CUT FROM THE SAME STORY. They're different butchering cuts from the same cow. Because the S8 narrative exists as a designed structure, any medium can extract its portion without losing coherence. The Twitch audience sees S0-S3 of one party. The podcast audience hears an edited version of the same events. The faction book reader sees the S5-S7 institutional context that caused those events. The journal entry reader sees an individual's S0-S2 perspective on the institutional operations described in the faction book.

Each medium tells a true story from a different Scope level. None contradicts the others because they all derive from the same outside-in architecture.

## Eliminating and Producing Information Bottlenecks

Because production is planned as a Scope stack, information bottlenecks can be DESIGNED rather than accidental:

**Deliberate withholding:** The Twitch audience doesn't see the S7 political context of their adventure. That information is withheld — it appears months later in a faction supplement. The audience experiences the same information degradation the CHARACTERS experienced. The withholding IS the dramatic structure.

**Deliberate revelation:** When the faction supplement finally reveals WHY the S3 briefing was wrong, the audience gets the "oh no" moment retroactively. They WATCHED the party walk into an ambush. Now they know who sent them and why the warning was missing. The revelation recontextualizes content they already consumed — increasing the value of BOTH the original content and the new revelation.

**Cross-media Easter eggs:** A name mentioned once in a Twitch session. The same name appears on an in-character document released on Patreon. The same name is a chapter heading in the faction book. Each appearance adds context without requiring the audience to have consumed the other media. But the audience that HAS consumed all three experiences a depth that single-medium audiences don't.

---

# PART IV: THE FIVE PRESENTATIONS

The content ecosystem requires five presentation layers that flow into each other without hard walls. A player reading talent descriptions encounters setting lore. A GM reading scenario design encounters player-facing rules. The interstitials are zones of overlap, not separate products.

## Presentation 1: The Player's Door

Character creation, core rules, "what do I do." The individual at S0. This is where someone walks in and starts playing.

**Content:** Attributes, skills, talents (combat petal), basic combat rules (tempo, FP, five degrees of success, condition tracks), equipment, the character sheet.

**Scope level:** S0-S2. The player understands their character and how their character interacts with immediate encounters.

**Design principle:** Everything here must be independently functional. A group that only ever reads Presentation 1 has a complete, playable game.

## Interstitial 1→2: The Institutional Interface

Where the individual player starts touching institutional systems. Commander talents, social combat, the Background system (Divine Umbrellas, Cultural Dissonance), guild contracts (Renown/Liability/Leverage), crowd combat, the investigation-to-strike pipeline.

**Scope level:** S3-S4. The player discovers that their actions have consequences beyond the immediate encounter. Their Background shifts. Their guild standing changes. The world responds.

**Design principle:** This content is accessible to players but primarily USED by players who engage with the social and institutional layers. A pure combat character can ignore this. A face, commander, or politically active character lives here.

## Presentation 2: The GM's Architecture

The Operating Philosophy, the Scope stack, the front/clock system, the outside-in scenario design methodology, the Zoetrope, the environmental engine.

**Scope level:** S4-S7. The GM understands WHY encounters happen, how institutional decisions create tactical situations, and how to build structurally coherent scenarios.

**Design principle:** Not walled off from players. A curious player can read this and understand why the world responds the way it does. But it's primarily the GM's toolkit for building campaigns that leverage the full Scope architecture.

## Interstitial 2→3: The Reference Layer

Spell lists, item catalogues, talent tables, crafting formulas, isotope profiles, the Elemental Cards, the Five Artifice Paths, college signature effects. The TOOLS both players and GMs use to interact with the setting.

**Scope level:** All Scopes. These are lookup tables for the language both sides speak.

**Design principle:** This is shared vocabulary, not player or GM content. A player looks up their spell's Construction Pool parameters. A GM looks up the isotope profile of the region. Both use the same reference material.

## Presentation 3: The Setting Bible

Tessera itself. The cosmology, the Great Lie, the Cold War, the colleges, the Fey, the Calamity, the environmental engine, the Zoetrope's full revolution. Readable as fiction independent of the mechanics.

**Scope level:** S7-S9. The story of what Tessera IS and why it exists.

**Design principle:** This is what someone reads and says "I want to play in THIS world" before they understand the rules. It sells the setting to people who may never play the game. It provides the S8 context that makes everything else meaningful.

---

# PART V: DEPLOYMENT STRATEGY

## The S8 Vision Deploys Over Time

The S8 narrative — the full story of Tessera from Calamity to whatever endpoint exists — is not revealed at launch. It emerges as content accumulates across media and time.

**Launch:** S0-S3 content. Core rulebook, starter adventure, first actual play sessions. The audience learns the setting by experiencing it at the most immediate Scope level. They know what Tessera FEELS like before they know what it IS.

**Year 1:** S0-S4 content accumulates. Multiple adventure arcs completed. Campaign modules released. The institutional layer becomes visible — the colleges, the guilds, the Zoetrope's political landscape. The audience starts asking "why is this happening?" and the S4-S5 supplements begin answering.

**Years 2-3:** S5-S6 content. Faction books, campaign setting supplements, the environmental engine made explicit. The audience can now see the mid-level Scope architecture. Cross-media connections become discoverable. The Chekhov's guns from Year 1 start paying off.

**Years 4-7:** S7-S8 content. The overarching narrative becomes visible. The setting's timeline advances. Major campaign arcs conclude and produce consequences that reshape the setting for future products. The audience that has followed from launch experiences the full Scope stack.

**Ongoing:** The S8 clock continues. New content enters at all Scope levels simultaneously. New audiences can enter at S0 at any time — the core rulebook and starter content remain the permanent entry point.

## The Nintendo Principle

Nintendo's long game is visible NOW because we can look backward. When designing forward, the principle is: each product must be INDEPENDENTLY VALUABLE to the person who buys it. The S8 vision provides structural coherence. The S0 execution provides immediate value.

The person who buys the core rulebook and never buys anything else has a complete game. The person who watches three Twitch sessions and never returns was entertained. The person who follows for seven years and consumes everything experiences a depth that justifies the investment. All three are valid audiences. None is underserved.

---

# PART VI: THE PRODUCTION FRAMEWORK AS CLOCK MANAGEMENT

## The Creator's Scope Stack

The solo creator (primary job + ÆC as second job) manages scope through batched production and backlog discipline. The production Scope stack is also a personal resource management system:

**S0 (daily):** Writing sessions, design conversations, iteration on specific mechanics. The raw creative work.

**S1 (weekly):** Batched output review. What was produced this week? What advances which clock?

**S2 (monthly):** Content batch completion. Is the monthly deliverable ready? Does it connect to the quarterly milestone?

**S3 (quarterly):** Milestone assessment. Does the quarterly output advance the semi-annual product? Course corrections happen here.

**S4-S5 (semi-annual to annual):** Product completion cycles. Major deliverables assessed against the multi-year arc.

The creator's own OODA cycles operate at these tempos. The temptation is to operate constantly at S0 (writing whatever feels urgent) without reference to higher Scope clocks. Backlog discipline means each S0 session is chosen because it advances a specific S1-S3 clock, which advances a specific S4-S5 product, which advances the S8 vision.

## The Managed Scope vs the Market Scope

The creator controls the production Scope stack. The market has its own Scope stack — audience attention cycles, industry trends, platform algorithm changes, competitor releases. These two stacks interact but don't synchronize.

The production framework must be robust to market Scope disruption — a platform algorithm change at S1 (weekly engagement) shouldn't derail the S5 (annual anchor) plan. The outside-in architecture provides this robustness: because the S8 vision is established, individual product decisions can adapt to market conditions without losing structural coherence.

The S0 Twitch format can change. The quarterly Patreon structure can adjust. The annual product can shift release windows. The S8 story doesn't change because it's not market-dependent — it's architecturally determined. The story of Tessera exists independent of the medium that carries it.

---

# PART VII: THE CORE QUESTION

## "How" vs "Why"

The system documents (Documents 1-5, talent system, environmental engine) answer "how do I swing my sword." The production framework answers "how do I build and sell the game."

Neither answers "why would I swing my sword." That answer lives in the narrative — the Marked of Aíon, the Great Lie, the Cold War, the personal stakes that make a character care enough to act. The narrative is the connective tissue between the mechanical system and the market strategy.

The mechanical system makes action POSSIBLE. The narrative makes action MEANINGFUL. The production framework makes the product ACCESSIBLE.

All three must exist. The system without narrative is a physics engine with no game. The narrative without system is a novel with no interactivity. The product without accessibility is a masterwork nobody finds.

The next phase of development — after the remaining system documents (Ancestry) and the Marked of Aíon narrative — addresses accessibility: the starter experience, the quickstart, the first session, the S0 engagement that determines whether someone ever sees S1.

---

*End of Production Framework. This document applies the Ætherium Codex's Scope system to its own production and market deployment, establishing the self-describing architecture that allows the game's design tools to serve double duty as business planning tools. The system describes itself.*

# THE ÆTHERIUM CODEX — SKILL × EFFECT COMPATIBILITY MATRIX
## Raw Bucket Assignment — Can This Skill Produce This Effect?
### Working Document — April 16, 2026

---

## METHOD

Each cell answers ONE question: can a trained practitioner using this skill produce this effect through technique alone (Tier 1-2), without constructing a Spell Sentence (Tier 3)?

**✓** = Yes, this skill can produce this effect through trained application.
**—** = No, this skill cannot produce this effect.

No hierarchy. No primary/secondary. Just compatibility.

---

## THE MATRIX

### PHYSICAL PILLAR SKILLS

| Effect | Athletics | Skullduggery | Conveyance | Survival |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **Damage** | ✓ Body slam, tackle, thrown object | ✓ Backstab, poison application, sneak attack | ✓ Ram, trample, vehicular weapon | ✓ Lethal traps, directing natural hazards |
| **Affliction** | ✓ Exhaustion through contest, pinning | ✓ Traps, poison, sabotage imposing conditions | ✓ Running target off road, forced dismount | ✓ Natural poisons, exposure conditions |
| **Nullify** | ✓ Physically blocking a flow, plugging breach | ✓ Disabling traps, picking locks, bypassing security | — | ✓ Countering hazards, neutralizing poisons with field remedies, disrupting predator patterns |
| **Weaken** | ✓ Wearing down through sustained exertion | ✓ Sabotaging equipment effectiveness | — | ✓ Denying resources, creating exposure conditions |
| **Protection** | ✓ Bracing, catching ally, body-blocking | ✓ Booby-trapping a position as defense | ✓ Evasive maneuvering, using vehicle as cover | ✓ Building shelter, finding safe positions, reading weather |
| **Immunity** | — | — | — | — |
| **Immortality** | — | — | — | — |
| **Movement** | ✓ Running, climbing, swimming, jumping | ✓ Stealthy movement, urban acrobatics | ✓ Vehicular/mounted movement | ✓ Pathfinding through difficult terrain |
| **Teleport** | — | — | ✓ Operating vehicle translocation systems | — |
| **Dimensional Travel** | — | — | ✓ Operating vehicle planar transit | — |

| **Mind Link** | — | — | — | — |
| **Feature** | — | — | — | — |
| **Chronos** | — | — | — | — |
| **Create** | — | ✓ Forging documents, constructing disguise props, building traps | — | ✓ Constructing shelters, snares, tools from natural materials |
| **Move Object** | ✓ Shoving, dragging, throwing, lifting | ✓ Sleight of hand, pickpocketing | ✓ Towing, pushing with vehicle | ✓ Redirecting natural forces (water flow, rockslide) |
